<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=edge">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>登录</title>

    <!--<link rel="stylesheet" href="./css/common.css">-->
    <!--<link rel="stylesheet" href="./css/login.css">-->
</head>
<body>
    <div class="nav">
        网络五子棋对战游戏
    </div>
    <div class="login-container">
        <!-- 登录界面的对话框 -->
        <div class="login-dialog">
            <!-- 提示信息 -->
            <h3>登录</h3>
            <!-- 这个表示一行 -->
            <div class="row">
                <span>用户名</span>
                <input type="text" id="user_name">
            </div>
            <!-- 这是另一行 -->
            <div class="row">
                <span>密码</span>
                <input type="password" id="password">
            </div>
            <!-- 提交按钮 -->
            <div class="row">
                <button id="submit" onclick="login()">提交</button>
            </div>
        </div>

    </div>

    <script src="./js/jquery.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        //1. 给按钮添加点击事件，调用登录请求函数
        //2. 封装登录请求函数
        function login() {
            //  1. 获取输入框中的用户名和密码，并组织json对象
            var login_info = {
                username: document.getElementById("user_name").value,
                password: document.getElementById("password").value
            };
            //  2. 通过ajax向后台发送登录验证请求
            $.ajax({
                url: "/login",
                type: "post",
                data: JSON.stringify(login_info),
                success: function(result) {
                    //  3. 如果验证通过，则跳转游戏大厅页面
                    alert("登录成功");
                    window.location.assign("/game_hall.html");
                },
                error: function(xhr) {
                    //  4. 如果验证失败，则提示错误信息，并清空输入框
                    alert(JSON.stringify(xhr));
                    document.getElementById("user_name").value = "";
                    document.getElementById("password").value = "";
                }
            })
        }

    </script>
</body>
</html>

<body>
    <div class="nav">
        网络五子棋对战游戏
    </div>
    <div class="login-container">
        <!-- 登录界面的对话框 -->
        <div class="login-dialog">
            <!-- 提示信息 -->
            <h3>登录</h3>
            <!-- 这个表示一行 -->
            <div class="row">
                <span>用户名</span>
                <input type="text" id="usr_name">
            </div>
            <!-- 这是另一行 -->
            <div class="row">
                <span>密码</span>
                <input type="password" id="passwd">
            </div>
            <!-- 提交按钮 -->
            <div class="row">
                <button id="submit" onclick="login()">提交</button>
            </div>
            <a class="register-link" href="http://120.79.179.55:7777/register.html">注册账号</a>
        </div>

    </div>

    <script src="./js/jquery.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";

        //1. 给按钮添加点击事件，调用登录请求函数
        //2. 封装登录请求函数
        function login() {
            //  1. 获取输入框中的用户名和密码，并组织json对象
            var login_info = {
                usr_name: document.getElementById("usr_name").value,
                passwd: document.getElementById("passwd").value
            };
            //  2. 通过ajax向后台发送登录验证请求
            $.ajax({
                url: SERVER_HTTP + "/login",
                type: "post",
                data: JSON.stringify(login_info),
                success: function(result) {
                    //  3. 如果验证通过，则跳转游戏大厅页面
                    alert("登录成功");
                    window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/hall.html");
                },
                error: function(xhr) {
                    //  4. 如果验证失败，则提示错误信息，并清空输入框
                    alert(JSON.stringify(xhr));
                    document.getElementById("usr_name").value = "";
                    document.getElementById("passwd").value = "";
                }
            })
        }

    </script>
</body>
</html>

        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";

        //1. 给按钮添加点击事件，调用登录请求函数
        //2. 封装登录请求函数
        function login() {
            //  1. 获取输入框中的用户名和密码，并组织json对象
            var login_info = {
                usr_name: document.getElementById("usr_name").value,
                passwd: document.getElementById("passwd").value
            };
            //  2. 通过ajax向后台发送登录验证请求
            $.ajax({
                url: SERVER_HTTP + "/login",
                type: "post",
                data: JSON.stringify(login_info),
                success: function(result) {
                    //  3. 如果验证通过，则跳转游戏大厅页面
                    alert("登录成功");
                    window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/hall.html");
                },
                error: function(xhr) {
                    //  4. 如果验证失败，则提示错误信息，并清空输入框
                    alert(JSON.stringify(xhr));
                    document.getElementById("usr_name").value = "";
                    document.getElementById("passwd").value = "";
                }
            })
        }

<body>
    <div class="nav">
        网络五子棋对战游戏
    </div>
    <div class="register-container">
        <!-- 注册界面的对话框 -->
        <div class="register-dialog">
            <!-- 提示信息 -->
            <h3>注册</h3>
            <!-- 这个表示一行 -->
            <div class="row">
                <span>用户名</span>
                <input type="text" id="usr_name">
            </div>
            <!-- 这是另一行 -->
            <div class="row">
                <span>密码</span>
                <input type="password" id="passwd">
            </div>
            <!-- 再确认一次密码 -->
            <div class="row">
                <span>确认密码</span>
                <input type="password" id="passwd_confirm">
            </div>
            <!-- 提交按钮 -->
            <div class="row">
                <button id="submit" onclick="register()">提交</button>
            </div>
            <a class="login-link" href="http://120.79.179.55:7777/login.html">已有账号，返回登录</a>
        </div>

    </div>

    <script src="./js/jquery.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";

        //1. 给按钮添加点击事件，调用注册请求函数
        //2. 封装注册请求函数
        function register() {
            //  1. 获取输入框中的用户名和密码，并组织json对象
            var passwd = document.getElementById("passwd").value;
            var passwd_confirm = document.getElementById("passwd_confirm").value;
            if(passwd != passwd_confirm) {
                alert("两次输入的密码不一致");
                document.getElementById("passwd").value = "";
                document.getElementById("passwd_confirm").value = "";
                return;
            }

            var register_info = {
                usr_name: document.getElementById("usr_name").value,
                passwd: passwd
            };
            //  2. 通过ajax向后台发送注册请求
            $.ajax({
                url: SERVER_HTTP + "/register",
                type: "post",
                data: JSON.stringify(register_info),
                success: function(result) {
                    //  3. 如果注册通过，则跳转游戏大厅页面
                    alert("注册成功");
                    window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/hall.html");
                },
                error: function(xhr) {
                    //  4. 如果注册失败，则提示错误信息，并清空密码输入框
                    alert(JSON.stringify(xhr));
                    document.getElementById("passwd").value = "";
                    document.getElementById("passwd_confirm").value = "";
                }
            })
        }

    </script>
</body>
</html>

            //  1. 获取输入框中的用户名和密码，并组织json对象

            var passwd_confirm = document.getElementById("passwd_confirm").value;

        <!-- 登录界面的对话框 -->
        <div class="login-dialog">
            <!-- 提示信息 -->
            <h3>登录</h3>
            <!-- 这个表示一行 -->
            <div class="row">
                <span>用户名</span>
                <input type="text" id="usr_name">
            </div>
            <!-- 这是另一行 -->
            <div class="row">
                <span>密码</span>
                <input type="password" id="passwd">
            </div>
            <!-- 提交按钮 -->
            <div class="row">
                <button id="submit" onclick="login()">提交</button>
            </div>
            <a class="register-link" href="http://120.79.179.55:7777/register.html">注册账号</a>
        </div>

    <script src="./js/jquery.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";

        //1. 给按钮添加点击事件，调用登录请求函数
        //2. 封装登录请求函数
        function login() {
            //  1. 获取输入框中的用户名和密码，并组织json对象
            var login_info = {
                usr_name: document.getElementById("usr_name").value,
                passwd: document.getElementById("passwd").value
            };
            //  2. 通过ajax向后台发送登录验证请求
            $.ajax({
                url: SERVER_HTTP + "/login",
                type: "post",
                data: JSON.stringify(login_info),
                success: function(result) {
                    //  3. 如果验证通过，则跳转游戏大厅页面
                    alert("登录成功");
                    window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/hall.html");
                },
                error: function(xhr) {
                    //  4. 如果验证失败，则提示错误信息，并清空输入框
                    alert(JSON.stringify(xhr));
                    document.getElementById("usr_name").value = "";
                    document.getElementById("passwd").value = "";
                }
            })
        }

    </script>

<body>
    <div class="nav">
        网络五子棋对战游戏
    </div>
    <div class="login-container">
        <!-- 登录界面的对话框 -->
        <div class="login-dialog">
            <!-- 提示信息 -->
            <h3>登录</h3>
            <!-- 这个表示一行 -->
            <div class="row">
                <span>用户名</span>
                <input type="text" id="usr_name">
            </div>
            <!-- 这是另一行 -->
            <div class="row">
                <span>密码</span>
                <input type="password" id="passwd">
            </div>
            <!-- 提交按钮 -->
            <div class="row">
                <button id="submit" onclick="login()">提交</button>
            </div>
            <a class="register-link" href="http://120.79.179.55:7777/register.html">注册账号</a>
        </div>

    </div>

    <script src="./js/jquery.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";

        //1. 给按钮添加点击事件，调用登录请求函数
        //2. 封装登录请求函数
        function login() {
            //  1. 获取输入框中的用户名和密码，并组织json对象
            var login_info = {
                usr_name: document.getElementById("usr_name").value,
                passwd: document.getElementById("passwd").value
            };
            //  2. 通过ajax向后台发送登录验证请求
            $.ajax({
                url: SERVER_HTTP + "/login",
                type: "post",
                data: JSON.stringify(login_info),
                success: function(result) {
                    //  3. 如果验证通过，则跳转游戏大厅页面
                    alert("登录成功");
                    window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/hall.html");
                },
                error: function(xhr) {
                    //  4. 如果验证失败，则提示错误信息，并清空输入框
                    alert(JSON.stringify(xhr));
                    document.getElementById("usr_name").value = "";
                    document.getElementById("passwd").value = "";
                }
            })
        }

    </script>
</body>
</html>

<body>
    <div class="nav">
        网络五子棋对战游戏
    </div>
    <div class="register-container">
        <!-- 注册界面的对话框 -->
        <div class="register-dialog">
            <!-- 提示信息 -->
            <h3>注册</h3>
            <!-- 这个表示一行 -->
            <div class="row">
                <span>用户名</span>
                <input type="text" id="usr_name">
            </div>
            <!-- 这是另一行 -->
            <div class="row">
                <span>密码</span>
                <input type="password" id="passwd">
            </div>
            <!-- 再确认一次密码 -->
            <div class="row">
                <span>确认密码</span>
                <input type="password" id="passwd_confirm">
            </div>
            <!-- 提交按钮 -->
            <div class="row">
                <button id="submit" onclick="register()">提交</button>
            </div>
            <a class="login-link" href="http://120.79.179.55:7777/login.html">已有账号，返回登录</a>
        </div>

    </div>

    <script src="./js/jquery.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";

        //1. 给按钮添加点击事件，调用注册请求函数
        //2. 封装注册请求函数
        function register() {
            //  1. 获取输入框中的用户名和密码，并组织json对象
            var passwd = document.getElementById("passwd").value;
            var passwd_confirm = document.getElementById("passwd_confirm").value;
            if(passwd != passwd_confirm) {
                alert("两次输入的密码不一致");
                document.getElementById("passwd").value = "";
                document.getElementById("passwd_confirm").value = "";
                return;
            }

            var register_info = {
                usr_name: document.getElementById("usr_name").value,
                passwd: passwd
            };
            //  2. 通过ajax向后台发送注册请求
            $.ajax({
                url: SERVER_HTTP + "/register",
                type: "post",
                data: JSON.stringify(register_info),
                success: function(result) {
                    //  3. 如果注册通过，则跳转游戏大厅页面
                    alert("注册成功");
                    window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/hall.html");
                },
                error: function(xhr) {
                    //  4. 如果注册失败，则提示错误信息，并清空密码输入框
                    alert(JSON.stringify(xhr));
                    document.getElementById("passwd").value = "";
                    document.getElementById("passwd_confirm").value = "";
                }
            })
        }

    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";

        //1. 给按钮添加点击事件，调用登录请求函数
        //2. 封装登录请求函数
        function login() {
            //  1. 获取输入框中的用户名和密码，并组织json对象
            var login_info = {
                usr_name: document.getElementById("usr_name").value,
                passwd: document.getElementById("passwd").value
            };
            //  2. 通过ajax向后台发送登录验证请求
            $.ajax({
                url: SERVER_HTTP + "/login",
                type: "post",
                data: JSON.stringify(login_info),
                success: function(result) {
                    //  3. 如果验证通过，则跳转游戏大厅页面
                    alert("登录成功");
                    window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/hall.html");
                },
                error: function(xhr) {
                    //  4. 如果验证失败，则提示错误信息，并清空输入框
                    alert(JSON.stringify(xhr));
                    document.getElementById("usr_name").value = "";
                    document.getElementById("passwd").value = "";
                }
            })
        }

(function (window) {
    function ajax(options) {
        var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
        var method = options.type || options.method || "GET";

        xhr.open(method.toUpperCase(), options.url, true);
        xhr.setRequestHeader("Content-Type", "application/json");

        xhr.onreadystatechange = function () {
            if (xhr.readyState !== 4) {
                return;
            }

            var result = xhr.responseText;
            try {
                result = JSON.parse(xhr.responseText);
            } catch (e) {
            }

            if (xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300) {
                if (options.success) {
                    options.success(result);
                }
            } else {
                if (options.error) {
                    options.error(xhr);
                }
            }
        };

        xhr.send(options.data || null);
    }

    window.$ = window.jQuery = {
        ajax: ajax
    };
})(window);

    <script>
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";

        //1. 给按钮添加点击事件，调用注册请求函数
        //2. 封装注册请求函数
        function register() {
            //  1. 获取输入框中的用户名和密码，并组织json对象
            var passwd = document.getElementById("passwd").value;
            var passwd_confirm = document.getElementById("passwd_confirm").value;
            if(passwd != passwd_confirm) {
                alert("两次输入的密码不一致");
                document.getElementById("passwd").value = "";
                document.getElementById("passwd_confirm").value = "";
                return;
            }

            var register_info = {
                usr_name: document.getElementById("usr_name").value,
                passwd: passwd
            };
            //  2. 通过ajax向后台发送注册请求
            $.ajax({
                url: SERVER_HTTP + "/register",
                type: "post",
                data: JSON.stringify(register_info),
                success: function(result) {
                    //  3. 如果注册通过，则跳转游戏大厅页面
                    alert("注册成功");
                    window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/hall.html");
                },
                error: function(xhr) {
                    //  4. 如果注册失败，则提示错误信息，并清空密码输入框
                    alert(JSON.stringify(xhr));
                    document.getElementById("passwd").value = "";
                    document.getElementById("passwd_confirm").value = "";
                }
            })
        }

    </script>

<body>
    <div class="nav">Online Gobang</div>
    <div class="hall-container">
        <div class="hall-dialog">
            <h3>Game Hall</h3>
            <div id="status" class="status">Connecting...</div>
            <button id="match_start" class="btn btn-start" onclick="startMatch()" disabled>Start Match</button>
            <button id="match_cancel" class="btn btn-cancel" onclick="cancelMatch()" disabled>Cancel Match</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        var ws = null;
        var matching = false;
        var checkRoomTimer = null;
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";
        var SERVER_WS = "ws://120.79.179.55:7777/ws";

        function wsUrl() {
            return SERVER_WS;
        }

        function setStatus(text) {
            document.getElementById("status").innerText = text;
        }

        function setButtons(canStart, canCancel) {
            document.getElementById("match_start").disabled = !canStart;
            document.getElementById("match_cancel").disabled = !canCancel;
        }

        function sendJson(data) {
            if(!ws || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {
                setStatus("Disconnected");
                setButtons(false, false);
                return;
            }
            ws.send(JSON.stringify(data));
        }

        function startMatch() {
            matching = true;
            setStatus("Matching...");
            setButtons(false, true);
            sendJson({ req_type: "match_start" });
            startCheckRoom();
        }

        function cancelMatch() {
            matching = false;
            stopCheckRoom();
            setStatus("Match cancelled");
            setButtons(true, false);
            sendJson({ req_type: "match_cancel" });
        }

        function startCheckRoom() {
            stopCheckRoom();
            checkRoomTimer = setInterval(function() {
                if(matching) {
                    sendJson({ req_type: "check_room", probe: true });
                }
            }, 1200);
        }

        function stopCheckRoom() {
            if(checkRoomTimer) {
                clearInterval(checkRoomTimer);
                checkRoomTimer = null;
            }
        }

        function handleMessage(resp) {
            if(resp.resp_type === "enter_hall") {
                if(resp.result) {
                    setStatus("In hall");
                    setButtons(true, false);
                } else {
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Enter hall failed");
                    setButtons(false, false);
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "match_start") {
                if(!resp.result) {
                    matching = false;
                    stopCheckRoom();
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Match failed");
                    setButtons(true, false);
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "match_cancel") {
                matching = false;
                stopCheckRoom();
                setStatus(resp.result ? "Match cancelled" : (resp.reason || "Cancel failed"));
                setButtons(true, false);
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "check_room" && resp.result) {
                stopCheckRoom();
                sessionStorage.setItem("room_info", JSON.stringify(resp.data || {}));
                window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/room.html");
            }
        }

        function connect() {
            ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl());
            ws.onopen = function() {
                setStatus("Connected, entering hall...");
                sendJson({ req_type: "enter_hall" });
            };
            ws.onmessage = function(event) {
                handleMessage(JSON.parse(event.data));
            };
            ws.onclose = function() {
                stopCheckRoom();
                setStatus("Connection closed");
                setButtons(false, false);
            };
            ws.onerror = function() {
                setStatus("Connection error");
                setButtons(false, false);
            };
        }

        window.onload = connect;
        window.onbeforeunload = function() {
            stopCheckRoom();
            if(ws && ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
                ws.close();
            }
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";

        //1. 给按钮添加点击事件，调用注册请求函数
        //2. 封装注册请求函数
        function register() {
            //  1. 获取输入框中的用户名和密码，并组织json对象
            var passwd = document.getElementById("passwd").value;
            var passwd_confirm = document.getElementById("passwd_confirm").value;
            if(passwd != passwd_confirm) {
                alert("两次输入的密码不一致");
                document.getElementById("passwd").value = "";
                document.getElementById("passwd_confirm").value = "";
                return;
            }

            var register_info = {
                usr_name: document.getElementById("usr_name").value,
                passwd: passwd
            };
            //  2. 通过ajax向后台发送注册请求
            $.ajax({
                url: SERVER_HTTP + "/register",
                type: "post",
                data: JSON.stringify(register_info),
                success: function(result) {
                    //  3. 如果注册通过，则跳转游戏大厅页面
                    alert("注册成功");
                    window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/hall.html");
                },
                error: function(xhr) {
                    //  4. 如果注册失败，则提示错误信息，并清空密码输入框
                    alert(JSON.stringify(xhr));
                    document.getElementById("passwd").value = "";
                    document.getElementById("passwd_confirm").value = "";
                }
            })
        }

    </script>

(function (window) {
    function ajax(options) {
        var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
        var method = options.type || options.method || "GET";

        xhr.open(method.toUpperCase(), options.url, true);
        xhr.setRequestHeader("Content-Type", "application/json");

        xhr.onreadystatechange = function () {
            if (xhr.readyState !== 4) {
                return;
            }

            var result = xhr.responseText;
            try {
                result = JSON.parse(xhr.responseText);
            } catch (e) {
            }

            if (xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300) {
                if (options.success) {
                    options.success(result);
                }
            } else {
                if (options.error) {
                    options.error(xhr);
                }
            }
        };

        xhr.send(options.data || null);
    }

    window.$ = window.jQuery = {
        ajax: ajax
    };
})(window);

            opacity: 0.6;
            transform: none;
            box-shadow: none;
        }

        .btn-start {
            background-color: #007bff;
        }

        .btn-cancel {
            background-color: #dc3545;
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="nav">Online Gobang</div>
    <div class="hall-container">
        <div class="hall-dialog">
            <h3>Game Hall</h3>
            <div id="status" class="status">Connecting...</div>
            <button id="match_start" class="btn btn-start" onclick="startMatch()" disabled>Start Match</button>
            <button id="match_cancel" class="btn btn-cancel" onclick="cancelMatch()" disabled>Cancel Match</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        var ws = null;
        var matching = false;
        var checkRoomTimer = null;
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";
        var SERVER_WS = "ws://120.79.179.55:7777/ws";

        function wsUrl() {
            return SERVER_WS;
        }

        function setStatus(text) {
            document.getElementById("status").innerText = text;
        }

        function setButtons(canStart, canCancel) {
            document.getElementById("match_start").disabled = !canStart;
            document.getElementById("match_cancel").disabled = !canCancel;
        }

        function sendJson(data) {
            if(!ws || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {
                setStatus("Disconnected");
                setButtons(false, false);
                return;
            }
            ws.send(JSON.stringify(data));
        }

        function startMatch() {
            matching = true;
            setStatus("Matching...");
            setButtons(false, true);
            sendJson({ req_type: "match_start" });
            startCheckRoom();
        }

        function cancelMatch() {
            matching = false;
            stopCheckRoom();
            setStatus("Match cancelled");
            setButtons(true, false);
            sendJson({ req_type: "match_cancel" });
        }

        function startCheckRoom() {
            stopCheckRoom();
            checkRoomTimer = setInterval(function() {
                if(matching) {
                    sendJson({ req_type: "check_room", probe: true });
                }
            }, 1200);
        }

        function stopCheckRoom() {
            if(checkRoomTimer) {
                clearInterval(checkRoomTimer);
                checkRoomTimer = null;
            }
        }

        function handleMessage(resp) {
            if(resp.resp_type === "enter_hall") {
                if(resp.result) {
                    setStatus("In hall");
                    setButtons(true, false);
                } else {
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Enter hall failed");
                    setButtons(false, false);
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "match_start") {
                if(!resp.result) {
                    matching = false;
                    stopCheckRoom();
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Match failed");
                    setButtons(true, false);
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "match_cancel") {
                matching = false;
                stopCheckRoom();
                setStatus(resp.result ? "Match cancelled" : (resp.reason || "Cancel failed"));
                setButtons(true, false);
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "check_room" && resp.result) {
                stopCheckRoom();
                sessionStorage.setItem("room_info", JSON.stringify(resp.data || {}));
                window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/room.html");
            }
        }

        function connect() {
            ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl());
            ws.onopen = function() {
                setStatus("Connected, entering hall...");
                sendJson({ req_type: "enter_hall" });
            };
            ws.onmessage = function(event) {
                handleMessage(JSON.parse(event.data));
            };
            ws.onclose = function() {
                stopCheckRoom();
                setStatus("Connection closed");
                setButtons(false, false);
            };
            ws.onerror = function() {
                setStatus("Connection error");
                setButtons(false, false);
            };
        }

        window.onload = connect;
        window.onbeforeunload = function() {
            stopCheckRoom();
            if(ws && ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
                ws.close();
            }
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";

        //1. 给按钮添加点击事件，调用登录请求函数
        //2. 封装登录请求函数
        function login() {
            //  1. 获取输入框中的用户名和密码，并组织json对象
            var login_info = {
                usr_name: document.getElementById("usr_name").value,
                passwd: document.getElementById("passwd").value
            };
            //  2. 通过ajax向后台发送登录验证请求
            $.ajax({
                url: SERVER_HTTP + "/login",
                type: "post",
                data: JSON.stringify(login_info),
                success: function(result) {
                    //  3. 如果验证通过，则跳转游戏大厅页面
                    alert("登录成功");
                    window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/hall.html");
                },
                error: function(xhr) {
                    //  4. 如果验证失败，则提示错误信息，并清空输入框
                    alert(JSON.stringify(xhr));
                    document.getElementById("usr_name").value = "";
                    document.getElementById("passwd").value = "";
                }
            })
        }

    </script>

    <script src="./js/jquery.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";

        //1. 给按钮添加点击事件，调用登录请求函数
        //2. 封装登录请求函数
        function login() {
            //  1. 获取输入框中的用户名和密码，并组织json对象
            var login_info = {
                usr_name: document.getElementById("usr_name").value,
                passwd: document.getElementById("passwd").value
            };
            //  2. 通过ajax向后台发送登录验证请求
            $.ajax({
                url: SERVER_HTTP + "/login",
                type: "post",
                data: JSON.stringify(login_info),
                success: function(result) {
                    //  3. 如果验证通过，则跳转游戏大厅页面
                    alert("登录成功");
                    window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/hall.html");
                },
                error: function(xhr) {
                    //  4. 如果验证失败，则提示错误信息，并清空输入框
                    alert(JSON.stringify(xhr));
                    document.getElementById("usr_name").value = "";
                    document.getElementById("passwd").value = "";
                }
            })
        }

    </script>
</body>

(function (window) {
    function ajax(options) {
        var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
        var method = options.type || options.method || "GET";

        xhr.open(method.toUpperCase(), options.url, true);
        xhr.setRequestHeader("Content-Type", "application/json");

        xhr.onreadystatechange = function () {
            if (xhr.readyState !== 4) {
                return;
            }

            var result = xhr.responseText;
            try {
                result = JSON.parse(xhr.responseText);
            } catch (e) {
            }

            if (xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300) {
                if (options.success) {
                    options.success(result);
                }
            } else {
                if (options.error) {
                    options.error(xhr);
                }
            }
        };

        xhr.send(options.data || null);
    }

    window.$ = window.jQuery = {
        ajax: ajax
    };
})(window);

<body>
    <div class="nav">Online Gobang</div>
    <div class="hall-container">
        <div class="hall-dialog">
            <h3>Game Hall</h3>
            <div id="status" class="status">Connecting...</div>
            <button id="match_start" class="btn btn-start" onclick="startMatch()" disabled>Start Match</button>
            <button id="match_cancel" class="btn btn-cancel" onclick="cancelMatch()" disabled>Cancel Match</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        var ws = null;
        var matching = false;
        var checkRoomTimer = null;
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";
        var SERVER_WS = "ws://120.79.179.55:7777/ws";

        function wsUrl() {
            return SERVER_WS;
        }

        function setStatus(text) {
            document.getElementById("status").innerText = text;
        }

        function setButtons(canStart, canCancel) {
            document.getElementById("match_start").disabled = !canStart;
            document.getElementById("match_cancel").disabled = !canCancel;
        }

        function sendJson(data) {
            if(!ws || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {
                setStatus("Disconnected");
                setButtons(false, false);
                return;
            }
            ws.send(JSON.stringify(data));
        }

        function startMatch() {
            matching = true;
            setStatus("Matching...");
            setButtons(false, true);
            sendJson({ req_type: "match_start" });
            startCheckRoom();
        }

        function cancelMatch() {
            matching = false;
            stopCheckRoom();
            setStatus("Match cancelled");
            setButtons(true, false);
            sendJson({ req_type: "match_cancel" });
        }

        function startCheckRoom() {
            stopCheckRoom();
            checkRoomTimer = setInterval(function() {
                if(matching) {
                    sendJson({ req_type: "check_room", probe: true });
                }
            }, 1200);
        }

        function stopCheckRoom() {
            if(checkRoomTimer) {
                clearInterval(checkRoomTimer);
                checkRoomTimer = null;
            }
        }

        function handleMessage(resp) {
            if(resp.resp_type === "enter_hall") {
                if(resp.result) {
                    setStatus("In hall");
                    setButtons(true, false);
                } else {
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Enter hall failed");
                    setButtons(false, false);
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "match_start") {
                if(!resp.result) {
                    matching = false;
                    stopCheckRoom();
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Match failed");
                    setButtons(true, false);
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "match_cancel") {
                matching = false;
                stopCheckRoom();
                setStatus(resp.result ? "Match cancelled" : (resp.reason || "Cancel failed"));
                setButtons(true, false);
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "check_room" && resp.result) {
                stopCheckRoom();
                sessionStorage.setItem("room_info", JSON.stringify(resp.data || {}));
                window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/room.html");
            }
        }

        function connect() {
            ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl());
            ws.onopen = function() {
                setStatus("Connected, entering hall...");
                sendJson({ req_type: "enter_hall" });
            };
            ws.onmessage = function(event) {
                handleMessage(JSON.parse(event.data));
            };
            ws.onclose = function() {
                stopCheckRoom();
                setStatus("Connection closed");
                setButtons(false, false);
            };
            ws.onerror = function() {
                setStatus("Connection error");
                setButtons(false, false);
            };
        }

        window.onload = connect;
        window.onbeforeunload = function() {
            stopCheckRoom();
            if(ws && ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
                ws.close();
            }
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script src="./js/jquery.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";

        //1. 给按钮添加点击事件，调用登录请求函数
        //2. 封装登录请求函数
        function login() {
            //  1. 获取输入框中的用户名和密码，并组织json对象
            var login_info = {
                usr_name: document.getElementById("usr_name").value,
                passwd: document.getElementById("passwd").value
            };
            //  2. 通过ajax向后台发送登录验证请求
            $.ajax({
                url: SERVER_HTTP + "/login",
                type: "post",
                data: JSON.stringify(login_info),
                success: function(result) {
                    //  3. 如果验证通过，则跳转游戏大厅页面
                    alert("登录成功");
                    window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/hall.html");
                },
                error: function(xhr) {
                    //  4. 如果验证失败，则提示错误信息，并清空输入框
                    alert(JSON.stringify(xhr));
                    document.getElementById("usr_name").value = "";
                    document.getElementById("passwd").value = "";
                }
            })
        }

    </script>

(function (window) {
    function ajax(options) {
        var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
        var method = options.type || options.method || "GET";

        xhr.open(method.toUpperCase(), options.url, true);
        xhr.setRequestHeader("Content-Type", "application/json");

        xhr.onreadystatechange = function () {
            if (xhr.readyState !== 4) {
                return;
            }

            var result = xhr.responseText;
            try {
                result = JSON.parse(xhr.responseText);
            } catch (e) {
            }

            if (xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300) {
                if (options.success) {
                    options.success(result);
                }
            } else {
                if (options.error) {
                    options.error(xhr);
                }
            }
        };

        xhr.send(options.data || null);
    }

    window.$ = window.jQuery = {
        ajax: ajax
    };
})(window);

<body>
    <div class="nav">Online Gobang</div>
    <div class="hall-container">
        <div class="hall-dialog">
            <h3>Game Hall</h3>
            <div id="status" class="status">Connecting...</div>
            <button id="match_start" class="btn btn-start" onclick="startMatch()" disabled>Start Match</button>
            <button id="match_cancel" class="btn btn-cancel" onclick="cancelMatch()" disabled>Cancel Match</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        var ws = null;
        var matching = false;
        var checkRoomTimer = null;
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";
        var SERVER_WS = "ws://120.79.179.55:7777/ws";

        function wsUrl() {
            return SERVER_WS;
        }

        function setStatus(text) {
            document.getElementById("status").innerText = text;
        }

        function setButtons(canStart, canCancel) {
            document.getElementById("match_start").disabled = !canStart;
            document.getElementById("match_cancel").disabled = !canCancel;
        }

        function sendJson(data) {
            if(!ws || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {
                setStatus("Disconnected");
                setButtons(false, false);
                return;
            }
            ws.send(JSON.stringify(data));
        }

        function startMatch() {
            matching = true;
            setStatus("Matching...");
            setButtons(false, true);
            sendJson({ req_type: "match_start" });
            startCheckRoom();
        }

        function cancelMatch() {
            matching = false;
            stopCheckRoom();
            setStatus("Match cancelled");
            setButtons(true, false);
            sendJson({ req_type: "match_cancel" });
        }

        function startCheckRoom() {
            stopCheckRoom();
            checkRoomTimer = setInterval(function() {
                if(matching) {
                    sendJson({ req_type: "check_room", probe: true });
                }
            }, 1200);
        }

        function stopCheckRoom() {
            if(checkRoomTimer) {
                clearInterval(checkRoomTimer);
                checkRoomTimer = null;
            }
        }

        function handleMessage(resp) {
            if(resp.resp_type === "enter_hall") {
                if(resp.result) {
                    setStatus("In hall");
                    setButtons(true, false);
                } else {
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Enter hall failed");
                    setButtons(false, false);
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "match_start") {
                if(!resp.result) {
                    matching = false;
                    stopCheckRoom();
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Match failed");
                    setButtons(true, false);
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "match_cancel") {
                matching = false;
                stopCheckRoom();
                setStatus(resp.result ? "Match cancelled" : (resp.reason || "Cancel failed"));
                setButtons(true, false);
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "check_room" && resp.result) {
                stopCheckRoom();
                sessionStorage.setItem("room_info", JSON.stringify(resp.data || {}));
                window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/room.html");
            }
        }

        function connect() {
            ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl());
            ws.onopen = function() {
                setStatus("Connected, entering hall...");
                sendJson({ req_type: "enter_hall" });
            };
            ws.onmessage = function(event) {
                handleMessage(JSON.parse(event.data));
            };
            ws.onclose = function() {
                stopCheckRoom();
                setStatus("Connection closed");
                setButtons(false, false);
            };
            ws.onerror = function() {
                setStatus("Connection error");
                setButtons(false, false);
            };
        }

        window.onload = connect;
        window.onbeforeunload = function() {
            stopCheckRoom();
            if(ws && ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
                ws.close();
            }
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>

            border-right: 0;

<body>
    <div class="nav">Online Gobang</div>
    <div class="page">
        <div class="panel">
            <h3>Game Room</h3>
            <div id="status" class="status">Connecting...</div>
            <div class="board-wrap">
                <div id="board" class="board"></div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="panel">
            <h3>Room Info</h3>
            <div class="info-line"><span>Room</span><strong id="room_id">-</strong></div>
            <div class="info-line"><span>Your Color</span><strong id="my_color">-</strong></div>
            <div class="info-line"><span>Current Turn</span><strong id="current_player">-</strong></div>

            <h3 style="margin-top: 24px;">Chat</h3>
            <div id="chat_list" class="chat-list"></div>
            <input id="chat_input" type="text" placeholder="Message">
            <button class="btn btn-send" onclick="sendChat()">Send Message</button>
            <button class="btn btn-exit" onclick="leaveRoom()">Leave Room</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        var ws = null;
        var roomInfo = {};
        var boardData = [];
        var gameover = false;
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";
        var SERVER_WS = "ws://120.79.179.55:7777/ws";

        function wsUrl() {
            return SERVER_WS;
        }

        function setStatus(text) {
            document.getElementById("status").innerText = text;
        }

        function colorText(color) {
            if(color === "black") return "Black";
            if(color === "white") return "White";
            return "-";
        }

        function playerText(uid) {
            if(!roomInfo || !uid) return "-";
            if(String(uid) === String(roomInfo.black_id)) return "Black";
            if(String(uid) === String(roomInfo.white_id)) return "White";
            return String(uid);
        }

        function sendJson(data) {
            if(!ws || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {
                setStatus("Disconnected");
                return;
            }
            ws.send(JSON.stringify(data));
        }

        function initBoard() {
            var board = document.getElementById("board");
            board.innerHTML = "";
            for(var row = 0; row < 15; row++) {
                for(var col = 0; col < 15; col++) {
                    var cell = document.createElement("div");
                    cell.className = "cell";
                    cell.dataset.row = row;
                    cell.dataset.col = col;
                    cell.onclick = function() {
                        putChess(Number(this.dataset.row), Number(this.dataset.col));
                    };
                    board.appendChild(cell);
                }
            }
        }

        function renderBoard() {
            var cells = document.querySelectorAll(".cell");
            for(var row = 0; row < 15; row++) {
                for(var col = 0; col < 15; col++) {
                    var cell = cells[row * 15 + col];
                    cell.classList.remove("black", "white");
                    var value = boardData[row] ? Number(boardData[row][col]) : 0;
                    if(value === 1) cell.classList.add("black");
                    if(value === 2) cell.classList.add("white");
                }
            }
        }

        function applyChess(data) {
            if(!data) return;
            var row = Number(data.row);
            var col = Number(data.col);
            if(!boardData[row]) boardData[row] = [];
            boardData[row][col] = data.color === "black" ? 1 : 2;
            renderBoard();

            if(data.result === "gameover") {
                gameover = true;
                setStatus(data.info || "Game over");
            } else {
                roomInfo.current_player = data.next_player;
                document.getElementById("current_player").innerText = playerText(data.next_player);
                setStatus("Waiting for " + playerText(data.next_player));
            }
        }

        function applyRoomInfo(data) {
            roomInfo = data || {};
            boardData = roomInfo.board || [];
            document.getElementById("room_id").innerText = roomInfo.room_id || "-";
            document.getElementById("my_color").innerText = colorText(roomInfo.color);
            document.getElementById("current_player").innerText = playerText(roomInfo.current_player);
            renderBoard();
            setStatus("Room ready, waiting for " + playerText(roomInfo.current_player));
        }

        function putChess(row, col) {
            if(gameover) {
                setStatus("Game already over");
                return;
            }
            sendJson({ req_type: "put_chess", row: row, col: col });
        }

        function appendChat(text, type) {
            var chatList = document.getElementById("chat_list");
            var line = document.createElement("div");
            line.className = "chat-line " + type;
            line.innerText = text;
            chatList.appendChild(line);
            chatList.scrollTop = chatList.scrollHeight;
        }

        function sendChat() {
            var input = document.getElementById("chat_input");
            var payload = input.value;
            if(!payload) return;
            sendJson({ req_type: "room_chat", payload: payload });
            input.value = "";
        }

        function leaveRoom() {
            sendJson({ req_type: "leave_room" });
            setTimeout(function() {
                window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/hall.html");
            }, 300);
        }

        function handleMessage(resp) {
            if(resp.resp_type === "check_room") {
                if(resp.result) {
                    applyRoomInfo(resp.data || {});
                } else {
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Room not found");
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "put_chess" || resp.resp_type === "peer_put_chess") {
                if(resp.result) {
                    applyChess(resp.data || {});
                } else {
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Put chess failed");
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "room_chat") {
                if(resp.result) {
                    appendChat("Me: " + ((resp.data || {}).payload || ""), "mine");
                } else {
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Send failed");
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "peer_room_chat") {
                appendChat("Peer: " + ((resp.data || {}).payload || ""), "peer");
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "leave_room") {
                window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/hall.html");
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "peer_leave_room") {
                gameover = true;
                setStatus(((resp.data || {}).info) || "Peer left");
            }
        }

        function connect() {
            ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl());
            ws.onopen = function() {
                setStatus("Connected, checking room...");
                sendJson({ req_type: "check_room" });
            };
            ws.onmessage = function(event) {
                handleMessage(JSON.parse(event.data));
            };
            ws.onclose = function() {
                setStatus("Connection closed");
            };
            ws.onerror = function() {
                setStatus("Connection error");
            };
        }

        function loadCachedRoomInfo() {
            var cached = sessionStorage.getItem("room_info");
            if(cached) {
                applyRoomInfo(JSON.parse(cached));
                sessionStorage.removeItem("room_info");
            }
        }

        document.getElementById("chat_input").addEventListener("keydown", function(event) {
            if(event.key === "Enter") {
                sendChat();
            }
        });

        initBoard();
        loadCachedRoomInfo();
        window.onload = connect;
        window.onbeforeunload = function() {
            if(ws && ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
                ws.close();
            }
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-CN">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=edge">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>Game Hall</title>
    <style>
        * {
            box-sizing: border-box;
        }

        body {
            font-family: 'Segoe UI', Tahoma, Geneva, Verdana, sans-serif;
            background-color: #f4f4f9;
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            align-items: center;
            justify-content: center;
            min-height: 100vh;
            margin: 0;
            color: #333;
        }

        .nav {
            margin-bottom: 24px;
            font-size: 28px;
            font-weight: bold;
            color: #333;
            text-align: center;
        }

        .hall-container {
            width: min(460px, calc(100% - 32px));
        }

        .hall-dialog {
            background: white;
            padding: 44px 46px;
            border-radius: 10px;
            box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
            text-align: center;
        }

        h3 {
            margin: 0 0 24px;
            font-size: 28px;
            color: #333;
        }

        .status {
            min-height: 24px;
            margin-bottom: 26px;
            color: #666;
            font-size: 15px;
        }

        .btn {
            width: 100%;
            min-height: 46px;
            margin-top: 12px;
            padding: 12px 30px;
            border: 0;
            border-radius: 5px;
            color: white;
            font-size: 18px;
            font-weight: bold;
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: transform 0.2s, box-shadow 0.2s, opacity 0.2s;
        }

        .btn:hover {
            transform: translateY(-2px);
            box-shadow: 0 4px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
        }

        .btn:disabled {
            cursor: not-allowed;
            opacity: 0.6;
            transform: none;
            box-shadow: none;
        }

        .btn-start {
            background-color: #007bff;
        }

        .btn-cancel {
            background-color: #dc3545;
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="nav">Online Gobang</div>
    <div class="hall-container">
        <div class="hall-dialog">
            <h3>Game Hall</h3>
            <div id="status" class="status">Connecting...</div>
            <button id="match_start" class="btn btn-start" onclick="startMatch()" disabled>Start Match</button>
            <button id="match_cancel" class="btn btn-cancel" onclick="cancelMatch()" disabled>Cancel Match</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        var ws = null;
        var matching = false;
        var checkRoomTimer = null;
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";
        var SERVER_WS = "ws://120.79.179.55:7777/ws";

        function wsUrl() {
            return SERVER_WS;
        }

        function setStatus(text) {
            document.getElementById("status").innerText = text;
        }

        function setButtons(canStart, canCancel) {
            document.getElementById("match_start").disabled = !canStart;
            document.getElementById("match_cancel").disabled = !canCancel;
        }

        function sendJson(data) {
            if(!ws || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {
                setStatus("Disconnected");
                setButtons(false, false);
                return;
            }
            ws.send(JSON.stringify(data));
        }

        function startMatch() {
            matching = true;
            setStatus("Matching...");
            setButtons(false, true);
            sendJson({ req_type: "match_start" });
            startCheckRoom();
        }

        function cancelMatch() {
            matching = false;
            stopCheckRoom();
            setStatus("Match cancelled");
            setButtons(true, false);
            sendJson({ req_type: "match_cancel" });
        }

        function startCheckRoom() {
            stopCheckRoom();
            checkRoomTimer = setInterval(function() {
                if(matching) {
                    sendJson({ req_type: "check_room", probe: true });
                }
            }, 1200);
        }

        function stopCheckRoom() {
            if(checkRoomTimer) {
                clearInterval(checkRoomTimer);
                checkRoomTimer = null;
            }
        }

        function handleMessage(resp) {
            if(resp.resp_type === "enter_hall") {
                if(resp.result) {
                    setStatus("In hall");
                    setButtons(true, false);
                } else {
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Enter hall failed");
                    setButtons(false, false);
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "match_start") {
                if(!resp.result) {
                    matching = false;
                    stopCheckRoom();
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Match failed");
                    setButtons(true, false);
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "match_cancel") {
                matching = false;
                stopCheckRoom();
                setStatus(resp.result ? "Match cancelled" : (resp.reason || "Cancel failed"));
                setButtons(true, false);
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "check_room" && resp.result) {
                stopCheckRoom();
                sessionStorage.setItem("room_info", JSON.stringify(resp.data || {}));
                window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/room.html");
            }
        }

        function connect() {
            ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl());
            ws.onopen = function() {
                setStatus("Connected, entering hall...");
                sendJson({ req_type: "enter_hall" });
            };
            ws.onmessage = function(event) {
                handleMessage(JSON.parse(event.data));
            };
            ws.onclose = function() {
                stopCheckRoom();
                setStatus("Connection closed");
                setButtons(false, false);
            };
            ws.onerror = function() {
                setStatus("Connection error");
                setButtons(false, false);
            };
        }

        window.onload = connect;
        window.onbeforeunload = function() {
            stopCheckRoom();
            if(ws && ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
                ws.close();
            }
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <div class="nav">Online Gobang</div>
    <div class="hall-container">
        <div class="hall-dialog">
            <h3>Game Hall</h3>
            <div id="status" class="status">Connecting...</div>
            <button id="match_start" class="btn btn-start" onclick="startMatch()" disabled>Start Match</button>
            <button id="match_cancel" class="btn btn-cancel" onclick="cancelMatch()" disabled>Cancel Match</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        var ws = null;
        var matching = false;
        var checkRoomTimer = null;
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";
        var SERVER_WS = "ws://120.79.179.55:7777/ws";

        function wsUrl() {
            return SERVER_WS;
        }

        function setStatus(text) {
            document.getElementById("status").innerText = text;
        }

        function setButtons(canStart, canCancel) {
            document.getElementById("match_start").disabled = !canStart;
            document.getElementById("match_cancel").disabled = !canCancel;
        }

        function sendJson(data) {
            if(!ws || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {
                setStatus("Disconnected");
                setButtons(false, false);
                return;
            }
            ws.send(JSON.stringify(data));
        }

        function startMatch() {
            matching = true;
            setStatus("Matching...");
            setButtons(false, true);
            sendJson({ req_type: "match_start" });
            startCheckRoom();
        }

        function cancelMatch() {
            matching = false;
            stopCheckRoom();
            setStatus("Match cancelled");
            setButtons(true, false);
            sendJson({ req_type: "match_cancel" });
        }

        function startCheckRoom() {
            stopCheckRoom();
            checkRoomTimer = setInterval(function() {
                if(matching) {
                    sendJson({ req_type: "check_room", probe: true });
                }
            }, 1200);
        }

        function stopCheckRoom() {
            if(checkRoomTimer) {
                clearInterval(checkRoomTimer);
                checkRoomTimer = null;
            }
        }

        function handleMessage(resp) {
            if(resp.resp_type === "enter_hall") {
                if(resp.result) {
                    setStatus("In hall");
                    setButtons(true, false);
                } else {
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Enter hall failed");
                    setButtons(false, false);
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "match_start") {
                if(!resp.result) {
                    matching = false;
                    stopCheckRoom();
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Match failed");
                    setButtons(true, false);
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "match_cancel") {
                matching = false;
                stopCheckRoom();
                setStatus(resp.result ? "Match cancelled" : (resp.reason || "Cancel failed"));
                setButtons(true, false);
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "check_room" && resp.result) {
                stopCheckRoom();
                sessionStorage.setItem("room_info", JSON.stringify(resp.data || {}));
                window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/room.html");
            }
        }

        function connect() {
            ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl());
            ws.onopen = function() {
                setStatus("Connected, entering hall...");
                sendJson({ req_type: "enter_hall" });
            };
            ws.onmessage = function(event) {
                handleMessage(JSON.parse(event.data));
            };
            ws.onclose = function() {
                stopCheckRoom();
                setStatus("Connection closed");
                setButtons(false, false);
            };
            ws.onerror = function() {
                setStatus("Connection error");
                setButtons(false, false);
            };
        }

        window.onload = connect;
        window.onbeforeunload = function() {
            stopCheckRoom();
            if(ws && ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
                ws.close();
            }
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>

<body>
    <div class="nav">Online Gobang</div>
    <div class="page">
        <div class="panel">
            <h3>Game Room</h3>
            <div id="status" class="status">Connecting...</div>
            <div class="board-wrap">
                <div id="board" class="board"></div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="panel">
            <h3>Room Info</h3>
            <div class="info-line"><span>Room</span><strong id="room_id">-</strong></div>
            <div class="info-line"><span>Your Color</span><strong id="my_color">-</strong></div>
            <div class="info-line"><span>Current Turn</span><strong id="current_player">-</strong></div>

            <h3 style="margin-top: 24px;">Chat</h3>
            <div id="chat_list" class="chat-list"></div>
            <input id="chat_input" type="text" placeholder="Message">
            <button class="btn btn-send" onclick="sendChat()">Send Message</button>
            <button class="btn btn-exit" onclick="leaveRoom()">Leave Room</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        var ws = null;
        var roomInfo = {};
        var boardData = [];
        var gameover = false;
        var SERVER_HTTP = "http://120.79.179.55:7777";
        var SERVER_WS = "ws://120.79.179.55:7777/ws";

        function wsUrl() {
            return SERVER_WS;
        }

        function setStatus(text) {
            document.getElementById("status").innerText = text;
        }

        function colorText(color) {
            if(color === "black") return "Black";
            if(color === "white") return "White";
            return "-";
        }

        function playerText(uid) {
            if(!roomInfo || !uid) return "-";
            if(String(uid) === String(roomInfo.black_id)) return "Black";
            if(String(uid) === String(roomInfo.white_id)) return "White";
            return String(uid);
        }

        function sendJson(data) {
            if(!ws || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {
                setStatus("Disconnected");
                return;
            }
            ws.send(JSON.stringify(data));
        }

        function initBoard() {
            var board = document.getElementById("board");
            board.innerHTML = "";
            for(var row = 0; row < 15; row++) {
                for(var col = 0; col < 15; col++) {
                    var cell = document.createElement("div");
                    cell.className = "cell";
                    cell.dataset.row = row;
                    cell.dataset.col = col;
                    cell.onclick = function() {
                        putChess(Number(this.dataset.row), Number(this.dataset.col));
                    };
                    board.appendChild(cell);
                }
            }
        }

        function renderBoard() {
            var cells = document.querySelectorAll(".cell");
            for(var row = 0; row < 15; row++) {
                for(var col = 0; col < 15; col++) {
                    var cell = cells[row * 15 + col];
                    cell.classList.remove("black", "white");
                    var value = boardData[row] ? Number(boardData[row][col]) : 0;
                    if(value === 1) cell.classList.add("black");
                    if(value === 2) cell.classList.add("white");
                }
            }
        }

        function applyChess(data) {
            if(!data) return;
            var row = Number(data.row);
            var col = Number(data.col);
            if(!boardData[row]) boardData[row] = [];
            boardData[row][col] = data.color === "black" ? 1 : 2;
            renderBoard();

            if(data.result === "gameover") {
                gameover = true;
                setStatus(data.info || "Game over");
            } else {
                roomInfo.current_player = data.next_player;
                document.getElementById("current_player").innerText = playerText(data.next_player);
                setStatus("Waiting for " + playerText(data.next_player));
            }
        }

        function applyRoomInfo(data) {
            roomInfo = data || {};
            boardData = roomInfo.board || [];
            document.getElementById("room_id").innerText = roomInfo.room_id || "-";
            document.getElementById("my_color").innerText = colorText(roomInfo.color);
            document.getElementById("current_player").innerText = playerText(roomInfo.current_player);
            renderBoard();
            setStatus("Room ready, waiting for " + playerText(roomInfo.current_player));
        }

        function putChess(row, col) {
            if(gameover) {
                setStatus("Game already over");
                return;
            }
            sendJson({ req_type: "put_chess", row: row, col: col });
        }

        function appendChat(text, type) {
            var chatList = document.getElementById("chat_list");
            var line = document.createElement("div");
            line.className = "chat-line " + type;
            line.innerText = text;
            chatList.appendChild(line);
            chatList.scrollTop = chatList.scrollHeight;
        }

        function sendChat() {
            var input = document.getElementById("chat_input");
            var payload = input.value;
            if(!payload) return;
            sendJson({ req_type: "room_chat", payload: payload });
            input.value = "";
        }

        function leaveRoom() {
            sendJson({ req_type: "leave_room" });
            setTimeout(function() {
                window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/hall.html");
            }, 300);
        }

        function handleMessage(resp) {
            if(resp.resp_type === "check_room") {
                if(resp.result) {
                    applyRoomInfo(resp.data || {});
                } else {
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Room not found");
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "put_chess" || resp.resp_type === "peer_put_chess") {
                if(resp.result) {
                    applyChess(resp.data || {});
                } else {
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Put chess failed");
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "room_chat") {
                if(resp.result) {
                    appendChat("Me: " + ((resp.data || {}).payload || ""), "mine");
                } else {
                    setStatus(resp.reason || "Send failed");
                }
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "peer_room_chat") {
                appendChat("Peer: " + ((resp.data || {}).payload || ""), "peer");
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "leave_room") {
                window.location.assign(SERVER_HTTP + "/hall.html");
                return;
            }

            if(resp.resp_type === "peer_leave_room") {
                gameover = true;
                setStatus(((resp.data || {}).info) || "Peer left");
            }
        }

        function connect() {
            ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl());
            ws.onopen = function() {
                setStatus("Connected, checking room...");
                sendJson({ req_type: "check_room" });
            };
            ws.onmessage = function(event) {
                handleMessage(JSON.parse(event.data));
            };
            ws.onclose = function() {
                setStatus("Connection closed");
            };
            ws.onerror = function() {
                setStatus("Connection error");
            };
        }

        function loadCachedRoomInfo() {
            var cached = sessionStorage.getItem("room_info");
            if(cached) {
                applyRoomInfo(JSON.parse(cached));
                sessionStorage.removeItem("room_info");
            }
        }

        document.getElementById("chat_input").addEventListener("keydown", function(event) {
            if(event.key === "Enter") {
                sendChat();
            }
        });

        initBoard();
        loadCachedRoomInfo();
        window.onload = connect;
        window.onbeforeunload = function() {
            if(ws && ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
                ws.close();
            }
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>

            border-radius: 5px;
            padding: 0 12px;
            font-size: 15px;
            color: #333;
            outline: none;
        }

        input:focus {
            border-color: #007bff;

            width: 100%;
